Theorem 1

Statement: For 102 parameters, the unique games instance satisfies the rigidity.

Proof: Suppose for contradiction that the statement fails. Then there exists a minimal counterexample by algebraic geometry methods in circuit complexity. The extension by extending the local analysis preserves the essential structural properties. ∎
